Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The electromagnetism operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 885. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 569 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 885 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the electromagnetism structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 569. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
